For structural design parameters such as internal pressure, tail size, and body stiffness, a fast, geometrically and materially nonlinear model is developed and validated. The fluid-structure interaction equations are solved implicitly with an efficient second-order box method. A pneumatic manifold robotic system is employed to actuate SLIDER in a quiescent water tank environment, allowing cross-comparison of computational and experimental results. We find that low-frequency swimming is dominated by resistant environmental forces, whereas higher-frequency swimming is primarily affected by inertial fluid forces. Using our efficient model alongside a genetic algorithm, we co-optimize a swimming control pattern and caudal fin design (subject to SLIDER's climbing morphology) to achieve a tethered swimming speed of 21.7 +/- 0.4 cm/s (0.59 Bl/s). Furthermore, we investigate the optimization procedure for a multimodal robot performing both swimming and climbing tasks.

</details>


### [113] [Embodied-R1.5: Evolving Physical Intelligence via Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11324)
*Yifu Yuan,Yaoting Huang,Xianze Yao,Yutong Li,Shuoheng Zhang,Linqi Han,Pengyi Li,Jiangeng Sun,Wenting Jia,Zhao Zhang,Yuhao Liu,Ruihao Liao,Yucheng Hu,Qiyu Wu,Yuxiao Li,Zibin Dong,Fei Ni,Yan Zheng,Shuyang Gu,Yi Ma,Hongyao Tang,Han Hu,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了Embodied-R1.5，一个8B参数的统一具身基础模型，通过自动数据构建和多任务平衡RL训练，在24个具身VLM基准中取得16个SOTA，并支持闭环规划-接地-校正框架，可微调为VLA在4个操作基准上超越π0.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身基础模型在推理能力（认知、规划、纠错、指向）上覆盖不足，且异构任务之间存在冲突，缺乏统一的架构来整合这些能力以迈向通用物理智能。

Method: 1) 三条自动化数据构建流水线，扩增15B token数据覆盖；2) 多任务平衡RL训练策略缓解异构任务冲突；3) Planner-Grounder-Corrector (PGC)闭环框架支持长程任务的自主执行与自纠错。

Result: 8B参数下在24个具身VLM基准中16个SOTA，超越Gemini-Robotics-ER-1.5和GPT-5.4；微调为VLA后在4个操作基准上超越π0.5；零样本真机实验验证了指令跟随、可操作性接地、铰接物体操作和长程复杂任务的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Embodied-R1.5证明了统一架构下整合多种具身推理能力的可行性，为具身基础模型提供了一条高效且可扩展的技术路径，开源模型、数据、代码和评估框架将推动该领域研究。

Abstract: We introduce Embodied-R1.5, a unified Embodied Foundation Model (EFM) that integrates comprehensive embodied reasoning capabilities, spanning embodied cognition, task planning, correction, and pointing, within a single architecture toward general physical intelligence. Leveraging three automated data construction pipelines to significantly expand the data coverage of critical capabilities, we build a large-scale data system of over 15B tokens, and design a multi-task balanced RL recipe to alleviate heterogeneous task conflicts. We further introduce a Planner-Grounder-Corrector (PGC) closed-loop framework that enables a single model to autonomously execute and self-correct over long-horizon tasks. With only 8B parameters, Embodied-R1.5 achieves SOTA on 16 out of 24 embodied VLM benchmarks, surpassing leading models like Gemini-Robotics-ER-1.5 and GPT-5.4. Benefiting from the internalized embodied capabilities, Embodied-R1.5 can be fine-tuned into a VLA with only a small amount of data, outperforming leading VLA models like $π_{0.5}$ across 4 popular manipulation benchmark suites. We further conduct extensive zero-shot real-robot experiments, validating performance in instruction following, affordance grounding, articulated object manipulation, and long-horizon complex tasks, demonstrating strong generalization to the physical world. We open-source model weights, datasets, training code, and EmbodiedEvalKit, an evaluation framework tailored for embodied tasks, to facilitate future research in EFMs.

</details>


### [114] [HiPi: Reproducible High-Fidelity Piezoresistive Sensors for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11372)
*Changyi Lin,Raihan Haque,Hui-Ping Wang,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiPi是一个可复现的高保真压阻式触觉传感系统，通过重新设计硬件堆栈（包括兼容商业PCB制造的紧凑型读出电路板、STM32微控制器模块、优化通信管线实现220Hz双机械臂2048触觉点读出、FPCB导电层简化传感器制造），在保持可复现性的同时显著提升触觉传感保真度，将IoU从0.428提升至0.797，Dice从0.539提升至0.886。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压阻式触觉传感器存在实用权衡：可复现的设计保真度低，而高保真读出架构难以制造、组装和部署。需要一种既易于复现又具备高保真读出的触觉传感系统，以推动密集压阻式触觉传感在双机械臂操作和多指机器人系统中的实际应用。

Method: 基于低串扰读出原理，重新设计完整硬件堆栈：1) 紧凑型读出PCB，兼容商业PCB制造和装配服务，消除手工焊接；2) 更小更低成本的STM32 MCU模块；3) 优化通信管线，在双机械臂四块密集触觉阵列（2048个触觉点）上实现220Hz读出；4) FPCB导电层简化传感器制造与堆叠。

Result: 使用3D打印结构化接触图案实验，HiPi相比可复现基线显著提升接触几何保真度：平均IoU从0.428提升至0.797，平均Dice分数从0.539提升至0.886。

Conclusion: HiPi弥合了可复现制造与高保真读出之间的重要差距，使密集压阻式触觉传感在双机械臂操作和多指机器人系统中更加实用。

Abstract: Piezoresistive tactile sensors are attractive for robotic manipulation because they are thin, lightweight, low-cost, and scalable to dense large-area sensing. However, existing systems still face a practical trade-off: recent reproducible designs emphasize accessibility and ease of reproduction, whereas high-fidelity readout architectures remain more difficult to fabricate, assemble, and deploy. We present HiPi, a reproducible high-fidelity piezoresistive sensing system for robotic manipulation. Building on a low-crosstalk readout principle, HiPi redesigns the complete hardware stack around reproducibility, deployability, and multi-sensor scalability. The system includes a compact readout PCB compatible with commercial PCB fabrication and assembly services, eliminating manual soldering; a smaller and lower-cost STM32-based MCU module; an optimized communication pipeline that achieves 220 Hz readout in a bimanual setup with four dense tactile arrays (2048 taxels in total); and FPCB-based conductive layers that simplify sensor fabrication and stacking. Experiments with structured 3D-printed contact patterns show that HiPi preserves contact geometry substantially better than a reproducible baseline, improving the average IoU from 0.428 to 0.797 and the average Dice score from 0.539 to 0.886. These results suggest that HiPi bridges an important gap between reproducible fabrication and high-fidelity readout, making dense piezoresistive tactile sensing more practical for bimanual manipulation and multi-fingered robotic systems.

</details>


### [115] [PLUME: Probabilistic Latent Unified World Modeling and Parameter Estimation for Multi-Finger Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11396)
*Abhinav Kumar,Soshi Iba,Rana Soltani Zarrin,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PLUME，一种概率潜在统一世界模型与参数估计方法，通过联合学习参数信念演化与条件动力学，在螺丝刀旋转等精密灵巧操作任务中实现sim-to-real零样本迁移，优于离线强化学习和世界模型增强行为克隆基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧操作高度依赖物理参数（形状、位姿、摩擦系数），仿真训练的策略在部署时面临参数不确定性。标准域随机化不足以应对需要根据具体参数调整策略的精密任务。

Method: PLUME：学习一个联合潜空间，同时表征多种定性不同的物理参数以及奖励；在线参数推理实现世界模型与真实动力学的有效对齐，无需重新训练或微调。

Result: 在仿真螺丝刀旋转、阀门旋转、桶提升、圆盘弹射任务以及硬件螺丝刀旋转任务中实现零样本迁移，超越SOTA离线强化学习和世界模型增强行为克隆基线。

Conclusion: 联合学习参数信念与条件动力学能够有效应对部署时的参数不确定性，实现精密灵巧操作的sim-to-real零样本迁移。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation with multi-finger hands can be sensitive to physical parameters such as object shape, pose, and friction coefficients. While simulation enables large-scale data collection with known parameter values, simulation-trained policies must still handle uncertainty at deployment, where the true parameters and therefore the true dynamics are unknown. Standard domain randomization strategies may be insufficient for precise tasks like screwdriver turning, as manipulation strategies may need to change depending on specific parameter values. To address this, we propose Probabilistic Latent Unified world Modeling and parameter Estimation (PLUME), a world model that jointly learns to evolve a belief over parameter values as well as the system dynamics conditioned on those parameters. We learn a latent space to jointly represent multiple qualitatively different physical parameters along with rewards, themselves functions of partially-observable variables, to inform planning. Our novel learning framework leads to efficient alignment of the world model to true dynamics through online parameter inference as opposed to re-training or fine-tuning. We evaluate our method on simulated screwdriver turning, valve turning, bucket lifting, and disk flicking tasks, as well as a hardware screwdriver turning task, where we achieve successful zero-shot transfer of our simulation-trained policy and outperform state-of-the-art offline reinforcement learning and world-model-augmented behavior cloning baselines. Please see our website at https://plume-world-model.github.io for videos.

</details>


### [116] [Dynamic Execution Horizon Prediction for Chunk-based Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11408)
*Yuchi Zhao,Miroslav Bogdanovic,Arjun Sohal,Liyu Tao,Kourosh Darvish,Alán Aspuru-Guzik,Florian Shkurti,Animesh Garg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出动态执行水平预测(DEHP)，通过在线强化学习训练轻量级执行水平预测分支，在完全冻结预训练chunk策略的前提下动态调整每次执行的动作步数，平衡开环chunk执行效率与闭环单步控制的反应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动作分块策略依赖固定的执行水平(horizon)，在精细操作任务中开环执行会导致反应不及时，而手动调参高度依赖任务且不灵活。

Method: 在预训练的分块策略基础上，使用在线强化学习训练一个轻量级执行水平预测分支，不修改原策略参数，动态预测每次应执行的动作步数。

Result: 在多种高精度和长时程操作任务上大幅提升成功率；定性分析显示精细阶段预测短horizon、自由空间运动预测长horizon，有效平衡效率与反应性。

Conclusion: DEHP是一种兼容黑盒分块策略、无需修改底层动作生成器的有效方法，通过动态调整执行水平显著提升机器人操作性能。

Abstract: Action chunking has become a standard design in modern robot policies, from diffusion/flow policies to vision-language-action models, where the policy predicts a sequence of actions and executes a fixed number of them instead of acting one step at a time. However, this paradigm relies on a key assumption: a fixed execution horizon. During chunk execution, the policy operates open-loop, which is particularly problematic for fine-grained manipulation tasks that require frequent replanning. In practice, the execution horizon is typically chosen through empirical tuning and is highly task-dependent. To this end, we propose Dynamic Execution Horizon Prediction (DEHP), an effective method that trains a lightweight execution-horizon prediction branch using online reinforcement learning while keeping the pretrained chunk policy completely frozen. This makes the method compatible with black-box chunk policies and isolates the effect of adapting the execution horizon from changes to the underlying action generator. Across our evaluations, DEHP improves the success rate of different high-precision and long-horizon manipulation tasks by a large margin. Our qualitative analysis further shows that DEHP predicts shorter execution horizons during fine-grained stages of the task and longer horizons during free-space motion. In this way, DEHP balances the efficiency of open-loop chunk execution with the reactivity of closed-loop single-step control. Project page: https://dehp-chunking.github.io/

</details>


### [117] [A Modular Dual-Camera Pipeline for Micro-Inspection Using Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11419)
*S. H. Mirtajadini,N. Rublein,R. M. Ramakrishnan,G. ter Maat,M. Aldibaja,A. Y. Mersha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种通用的无人机微距巡检管线（aerial_micro_inspection），利用变焦云台相机+宽视场立体导航相机的双摄像头配置，结合视觉反馈回路补偿无人机运动，实现对树木、温室等非结构目标的高覆盖、高细节巡检，并在仿真与实物实验中验证了有效性，代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机巡检系统需飞近目标、轨迹规划依赖先验信息、窄视野易丢失目标，难以应对树木/车辆/人等非结构目标；同时无人机受扰流和定位误差影响大。

Method: 双摄像头方案：变焦云台相机用于远距离拍摄细节，宽视场立体导航相机用于在线获取目标表面、估计距离并分割成小巡检区域；视觉反馈回路补偿无人机运动，确保微小区域覆盖；管线基于ROS 2，模块化设计（表面分割+微目标检测可替换）。

Result: 仿真实验证明在扰动下覆盖鲁棒性提升；实物实验成功检测橡树上的橡树行军虫及卵、温室内粘虫板上的粉虱，实现高细节成像。

Conclusion: 该开源管线适用于多种无人机微距巡检场景，无需目标先验信息，对干扰具有鲁棒性，且易于迁移至新应用。

Abstract: Most existing drone-based inspection systems require the drone to fly dangerously close to the target or follow complex flight paths to capture small details. In addition, drone flight is affected by disturbances and localization inaccuracies, which can cause the drone to lose sight of its supposed target when it has a narrow view. Furthermore, trajectory planning often requires prior information about the target's geometry, position, and orientation, which is not always available for non-structural targets such as trees, vehicles, or people. To address these challenges, this paper presents aerial_micro_inspection, a generic pipeline for aerial micro-inspection across different use cases. The pipeline assumes a PX4-powered drone equipped with two cameras: (i) a zoomed, gimbal-mounted inspection camera that captures fine details without requiring the drone to fly very close to the target, and (ii) a wide-field-of-view stereo navigation camera that acquires the target surface on site, estimates its range, and partitions it into smaller inspection regions. In addition, a vision-based feedback loop compensates for drone motion while the inspection camera visits small partitions of a larger surface. We evaluate the pipeline in simulation and real-world experiments, mainly in two use-case scenarios: tree inspection for detecting oak processionary caterpillars and their eggs, and greenhouse inspection of sticky traps for detecting whiteflies. The results show improved coverage robustness under drone disturbances in simulation, as well as effective detection of caterpillars and eggs and high-detail imaging of insects in real-world experiments. The pipeline is open-source, developed in ROS 2, and can be adapted to new applications by replacing the surface-segmentation and micro-target detection checkpoints. The code is available at: https://github.com/SaxionMechatronics/aerial_micro_inspection

</details>


### [118] [Bridging the sim2real gap in the table tennis robot with a transformer-based ball states predictor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11464)
*Yin Bi,Christian Conti,Bilan Yang,Alexander Sigrist,Peter Dürr,Naoya Takahashi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于Transformer的乒乓球状态预测框架，利用注意力机制直接从历史观测建模长程时间依赖，无需显式物理模型；同时引入SPAD策略（用真实世界训练的预测器替代物理模拟器）实现高效的sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理方法依赖精确参数辨识和初始状态，学习方法难以捕捉长程时序依赖且通常受限于有限或模拟数据，亟需一种能利用大规模真实数据、准确预测长程球状态的方案。

Method: 1) 构建Transformer架构，通过注意力机制直接从历史观测预测球状态，不依赖显式飞行/弹跳模型；2) 收集涵盖不同技能水平和球炮配置的大规模真实世界数据集；3) 提出SPAD策略，在部署时用真实世界训练的预测器替换训练时用的物理模拟器，无需重新训练策略。

Result: Transformer+大规模真实数据实现准确的长程预测；SPAD策略有效缩小sim-to-real差距，同时保持基于模拟训练的效率和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 简单替换部署端的预测器即可显著改善sim-to-real迁移，无需重新训练策略，为高速动态环境中的闭环控制提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Robotic table tennis is a representative benchmark for high-speed, closed-loop robotic control in dynamic environments, where accurate and fast prediction of ball states is critical for reliable planning and control. Physics-based approaches rely heavily on accurate parameter identification and precise initial state, while learning-based methods often struggle to capture long-range temporal dependencies and are typically trained on limited or simulated data. We propose a transformer-based framework for table tennis ball state prediction that leverages attention mechanisms to model long-range temporal correlations directly from historical observations, without relying on explicit flight or bounce models. To support robust learning and generalization, we collected a large-scale real-world dataset from players of varying skill levels and diverse ball cannon configurations. The combination of a high-capacity transformer architecture and extensive real-world data enables accurate long-horizon forecasting. Building on this capability, we introduce a plug-and-play sim-to-real transfer strategy, Swap Predictor at Deployment (SPAD), which replaces the physics-based simulator used during training with the proposed real-world-trained predictor at deployment, improving the sim-to-real transferability of the policy without requiring retraining. We demonstrate that this simple substitution effectively narrows the sim-to-real gap while preserving the efficiency and scalability of simulation-based training.

</details>


### [119] [Steering Multirobot Behavior via Closed-Loop Affine Activation Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11489)
*Satyajeet Das,Darren Chiu,Shashank Hegde,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CLAE（闭环仿射激活编辑）框架，在推理时通过编辑冻结策略的中间激活来引导机器人行为，无需微调或重训练。在冻结的多四旋翼导航策略上验证了三种行为编辑能力：个体速度控制、多机编队保持、规避监控摄像头的新行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人需在预训练策略包络之外适应新行为，但策略微调或重训练存在灾难性遗忘风险，会降低预训练策略的基线性能。现有推理时编辑方法多用于语言模型，缺乏适用于机器人策略的闭环、状态依赖的激活编辑方案。

Method: 三步流程：1) 在冻结策略的中间激活上训练稀疏自编码器（SAE）；2) 通过事后探针选择与目标行为相关的潜在特征；3) 学习一个轻量级基于RL的引导策略，在推理时对选中的潜在表示施加状态依赖的仿射编辑（缩放+偏移）。整个过程中基础策略权重和动作头保持不变。

Result: 在仿真和实物实验中，CLAE能在保持原始导航任务性能的同时实现：1) 个体机器人速度曲线控制；2) 多机器人编队保持；3) 规避监控摄像机的新行为。证明冻结策略可通过激活编辑获得超越原训练任务范围的行为能力。

Conclusion: CLAE提供了一种无需改变策略权重的行为引导范式，通过推理时激活编辑实现自适应行为调整，有效避免灾难性遗忘，并在多机器人系统中展示出良好的可扩展性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Real-world robots need to adapt their behavior beyond the envelope of their pre-trained policy. Policy finetuning or retraining are options, but they risk catastrophic forgetting, degrading the pretrained policy's base performance. To combat this, we introduce CLAE: Closed-Loop Affine Activation Editing, an inference-time framework for steering the behavior of a frozen policy by editing intermediate activations while keeping the base policy weights and downstream action head untouched. CLAE approaches behavior steering as a closed-loop problem whose outputs edit policy activations that adapt online to the robot state, environment, target behavior, and multi-robot context. It trains a sparse autoencoder over frozen-policy activations, selects behavior-relevant latent features via post-hoc probing, and learns a lightweight RL-based steering policy that applies state-dependent affine edits to selected latents during inference. We validate CLAE on a frozen multi-quadrotor navigation policy trained to perform a single task: navigating robots to a set of goal locations while avoiding obstacles. Through extensive simulations and physical tests, we show that while navigating to their goal positions, CLAE can 1. steer individual robot behavior by controlling each robot's velocity profile; 2. coordinate multirobot behavior by preserving a desired formation; and 3. produce entirely new behavior wherein robots are required to reduce their exposure to surveillance cameras in the environment.

</details>


### [120] [Learning Object Manipulation from Scratch via Contrastive Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11525)
*Tongle Shen,Caleb Chuck,Fan Feng,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出交互加权重采样(IWR)方法，通过交互感知的重采样改善对比强化学习(CRL)在操作任务（尤其是涉及接触/抓取等交互诱导模式变化的场景）中的表示学习能力，在仿真和真实机器人空气曲棍球任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CRL在交互密集的操作任务中表现不佳，因为物体交互（如接触、抓取）会导致动态模式的突变，产生分段非线性的可达性结构，而现有CRL能量函数难以刻画和规划这种结构。

Method: 将操作动力学建模为分段平滑马尔可夫过程，提出Interaction-weighted Resampling (IWR)：在交互前、中、后三个阶段进行交互感知的重采样，促使学习到的表示保留决定未来可达性的模式边界，从而捕获多模态和分段非线性可达性。

Result: 在2D动态控制、机器人操作和空气曲棍球环境中均优于先前CRL方法，仿真平均提升19.8%；在真实机器人空气曲棍球任务中首次实现目标条件策略，成功率从25%提升到60%。

Conclusion: IWR通过显式处理交互诱导的模式变化，有效提升了CRL在操作任务中的表示学习和规划能力，并展示了从仿真到真实世界的可迁移性。

Abstract: Contrastive Reinforcement Learning (CRL) has seen recent success in a wide variety of goal-conditioned robotics tasks by learning structured representations of the dynamics. However, despite its success in locomotion and simpler control domains, CRL often struggles in interaction-rich manipulation. We argue that a key source of this difficulty is object-centric interaction, such as contact or grasping, that induces distinct changes in the underlying dynamic modes. In this work, we formulate manipulation dynamics as a piecewise-smooth Markov process and show that interaction-induced mode changes create piecewise nonlinear reachability structures that are difficult for standard CRL energy functions to represent and plan over. Based on this analysis, we introduce Interaction-weighted Resampling (IWR). IWR performs interaction-aware resampling around phases before, during, and after interactions, encouraging the learned representation to preserve the mode boundaries that determine future reachability to capture multi-modal and piecewise nonlinear reachability. Across interaction-centric environments, including 2D dynamic control, robotic manipulation, and robot air hockey, IWR improves both sample efficiency and overall performance over prior CRL methods, with 19.8% average improvement in simulation. Finally, using a sim-to-real pipeline with policies trained by IWR, we demonstrate the first real-world goal-conditioned robot air hockey agent capable of hitting goals, improving success from 25% to 60%. Project Page: IWR-arxiv.github.io.

</details>


### [121] [Adversarial Attacks on Learned Policies for Surgical Robotic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11535)
*Shutong Jin,Ziyang Chen,Preethi Satish,Paavan Gupta,Florian T. Pokorny,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首次系统研究了机器人辅助手术中基于学习的策略（ACT、Diffusion Policy、Pi0）对 adversarial attacks（破坏性攻击和引导性攻击）的脆弱性，提出三种攻击方法和新型光度对抗攻击，560次物理实验表明手术子任务成功率平均下降61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的策略正被用于增强手术机器人灵巧性，但端到端视觉-动作映射可能受到对抗攻击导致患者受伤，需要揭示此类安全威胁。

Method: 提出三种对抗攻击方法（逐步放宽对策略信息的访问权限）和一种模拟自然光照变化的光度对抗攻击，在清创和缝合两个手术子任务上评估对ACT、Diffusion Policy、Pi0三种策略架构的影响。

Result: 560次物理实验中，最先进的手术策略被显著干扰，子任务成功率平均下降61%。

Conclusion: 手术机器人中的学习策略面临严峻的对抗安全威胁，需要开发防御机制来保障患者安全。

Abstract: Learning-based policies are being considered to augment the dexterity of human surgeons in robot-assisted surgery. Can the end-to-end mapping from visual observations to robot actions be vulnerable to adversarial attacks, potentially leading to patient injury? In this paper, we present the first study of adversarial threats to learning-based policies in surgical robotics. We investigate two threat modes: (a) disruptive attacks, where imperceptible visual perturbations interrupt policy execution, and (b) steering attacks, where such perturbations steer policy actions toward attacker-specified directions. We formulate three adversarial attack methods, each with increasing access to policy information, and evaluate their impact on two surgical subtasks: debridement and suturing. Our evaluation covers three end-to-end policy architectures: ACT, Diffusion Policy, and Pi0. In addition, we introduce a new class of photometric adversarial attacks that mimic natural visual changes, such as lighting variations, to generate effective yet visually plausible perturbations. Results from 560 physical experiments using phantoms for debridement and suturing suggest that state-of-the-art policies can be significantly disrupted, resulting in an average 61% reduction in surgical subtask success rates. Project page: https://sites.google.com/view/adversary-surgery

</details>


### [122] [ConsistencyPlanner: Real-time Planning with Fast-Sampling Consistency Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11569)
*Qichao Zhang,Xing Fang,Jiaqi Fang,Zhenwen Cai,Jie Ling,Qiankun Yu,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Consistency Planner，一种基于快速采样一致性模型的实时规划框架，通过高效多模态采样和异构特征融合提升自动驾驶闭环规划的安全性和实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习驱动的自动驾驶规划方法难以同时兼顾多模态驾驶行为建模与实时规划，常导致决策犹豫或不安全行为。

Method: 1) 采用快速采样一致性模型生成多样化未来轨迹，实现高效多模态采样；2) 引入注意力增强解码器，动态融合场景特征和动作token等异构输入特征。

Result: 在Waymax仿真器中，相比现有方法在安全指标上取得显著提升，尤其在挑战性动态场景中表现优异。

Conclusion: 一致性模型结合异构特征融合可有效解决自动驾驶实时规划中多模态与实时性的权衡问题。

Abstract: Closed-loop planning in complex, real-world driving scenarios presents a critical challenge for autonomous driving systems. While traditional rule-based methods are interpretable, their predefined heuristics lack the adaptability for dynamic traffic environments. Learning-based approaches have shown considerable promise. Conversely, learning-based approaches, despite their promise, struggle to balance the modeling diverse and multimodal driving behaviors and real-time planning, often leading to indecisive or unsafe actions. To address this limitation, we propose Consistency Planner, a real-time planning framework with fast-sampling consistency models. Our approach is built upon two key technical contributions. Efficient Multimodal Sampling: We employ fast-sampling consistency models to generate a diverse set of plausible future trajectories. This enables efficient, real-time exploration of multimodal actions, overcoming the computational bottlenecks of previous iterative generative methods. Heterogeneous Feature Fusion: We introduce an attention-enhanced decoder that dynamically integrates heterogeneous input features (including scene feature and action token) into a cohesive representation for robust planning. Extensive evaluation in the Waymax simulator demonstrates superior performance in safety metrics compared to existing methods, with particularly strong results in challenging dynamic scenarios.

</details>


### [123] [Distortion-Resilient Robotic Imitation Learning for Autonomous Cable Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11577)
*Hao Wang,Fu-Zhao Ou,Shiqi Wang,Zhaolin Wan,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合图像质量评估模块和置信度学习机制的机器人模仿学习框架，用于在图像观测失真条件下保持高性能决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业中的电缆布线任务常面临图像观测失真问题，现有智能控制方法未专门针对图像信号失真场景设计，且图像质量信息未被充分利用来提升控制性能。

Method: 框架包含三个模块：图像质量评估模块（提取图像质量信息）、置信度学习机制（自适应优先处理困难样本）和决策模块（输出离散技能或连续动作），三者协同工作。

Result: 实验结果表明该框架能提升决策模块的整体性能，尤其在失真图像观测下保持高性能。

Conclusion: 融合图像质量信息与置信度学习可有效提升机器人在图像失真场景下的模仿学习性能。

Abstract: The rapid development of intelligent control methodologies has endowed robots with powerful autonomous intelligence. Cable routing, a ubiquitous foundational task in industry, provides a rigorous benchmark for robotic dexterity and sequential decision-making. In these practical scenarios, image observation distortion frequently occurs. Samples characterized by low-quality image observations often hinder accurate model training, posing challenges to the reliability and accuracy of intelligent control systems. Nevertheless, no dedicated intelligent control solution has been proposed for scenarios of image signal distortion. Meanwhile, image quality information has not been sufficiently exploited to further enhance the performance of intelligent control methodologies. To this end, we propose a novel robotic imitation learning framework that comprises an image quality assessment module, a confidence-based learning mechanism, and a decision-making module, which is designed to maintain high performance even under distorted image observations. In the proposed framework, the image quality assessment module synergizes with the confidence-based learning mechanism to enhance the efficacy of the decision-making module. Specifically, the image quality assessment module is incorporated to extract image quality information from image observations, while the confidence-based learning mechanism adaptively prioritizes challenging samples to improve learning effectiveness. The decision-making module determines appropriate discrete skills or continuous actions. Experimental results demonstrate that our formulated framework enhances the overall performance of the decision-making module.

</details>


### [124] [LUCID: Learning Embodiment-Agnostic Intent Models from Unstructured Human Videos for Scalable Dexterous Robot Skill Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11628)
*Harsh Gupta,Guanya Shi,Wenzhen Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LUCID是一个两阶段框架，从互联网规模的非结构化人类视频中学习任务意图（短期场景预测），再通过仿真训练的感知运动策略将该意图映射为具体机器人动作。意图模型跨本体通用，支持灵巧手和夹爪等多种具身形态，在搅拌、擦拭、分拣等任务上实现了零样本迁移到新场景和新物体，仅需互联网视频或少量自拍视频监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习依赖昂贵的机器人示范或结构化人类数据，难以规模化且绑定特定具身形态。未结构化人类视频虽然丰富但无法直接映射到机器人动作，缺乏连接高层任务意图与底层控制的通用接口。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 意图模型（intent model）从互联网视频（Ego4D等）中学习，以当前观测预测短时域的场景变化意图（如物体下一步应发生什么变化）；2) 本体特定的感知运动策略在并行仿真中训练，将意图解码为具体机器人动作。意图接口作为跨本体的共享中间表征。

Result: 在五个真实操作任务上验证：搅拌、擦拭、分拣（仅靠互联网视频监督，零样本泛化到新场景和新物体）；推-T和线缆路由（各1小时自拍手机视频）。灵巧手和夹爪两种本体均有效。

Conclusion: LUCID成功利用互联网量级非结构化视频学习可迁移的任务意图，通过意图-控制的分离实现跨本体泛化，为规模化机器人学习提供了不依赖昂贵示范数据的可行路径。

Abstract: The most widely-adopted robot learning pipelines today learn skills from robot demonstrations or structured human data, which are expensive to collect and tied to specific embodiments. In contrast, unstructured human videos provide a scalable alternative. They contain diverse manipulation demonstrations across objects, scenes, and strategies, but are not directly connected to robot action. We propose LUCID, a two-stage framework that learns task intent from unstructured human videos drawn from internet-scale datasets and learns robot control in massively-parallel simulation. The intent model predicts short-horizon intent (what should happen next in the scene) from the current observation in closed loop. An embodiment-specific sensorimotor policy converts this intent into robot actions. The intent interface is shared across controllers, so the same intent model can be applied to different embodiments, from our primary dexterous hand to a parallel-jaw gripper. We evaluate LUCID on five real-world manipulation tasks: stirring, wiping, and binning supervised by only internet video, with zero-shot transfer to novel scenes and object instances; and push-T and cable routing supervised by 1 hr each of self-collected smartphone video. Project page: https://lucid-robot.github.io/.

</details>


### [125] [SAFER-Nav: Enhancing Safety for Visual Robot Navigation via Segmentation-Aware Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11636)
*Geonyeong Ko,Giung Lee,Changjoo Nam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种通过微调将障碍边界和可通行自由空间结构显式纳入导航策略的方法，兼容多种RGB骨干网络，在多个机器人平台和场景中降低碰撞频率，同时保持到达目标的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉导航模型（如ViNT、NoMaD）在陌生环境中泛化能力差，生成的轨迹虽有目标导向但不安全，且未显式学习障碍边界或可通行空间结构。

Method: 通过微调将障碍边界和可通行自由空间结构直接融入导航策略，设计为兼容多种RGB骨干网络。

Result: 在多种机器人平台、室内环境、静态和动态障碍场景下，相比ViNT、NoMaD及其CARE增强变体，降低了碰撞频率，同时保持目标到达性能。

Conclusion: 显式建模障碍边界和自由空间结构能有效提升视觉导航模型在陌生环境中的安全性，且该方法具有良好的骨干网络兼容性。

Abstract: Vision-based navigation models, particularly foundation models, generate viable trajectories from RGB observations alone. However, even state-of-the-art transformer- and diffusion-based policies struggle to generalize in unfamiliar deployment environments containing unseen obstacles or shifted conditions. The resulting trajectories often remain goal-directed but unsafe. Existing efforts improve safety through external trajectory correction or internal geometric priors, yet the resulting policies are not trained to explicitly represent obstacle boundaries or traversable free-space structure. To address this, we propose a navigation model that incorporates these structures directly into the policy via fine-tuning and is designed to be compatible with diverse RGB-based backbones. Across multiple robot platforms, indoor environments, and static and dynamic obstacle scenarios, our method reduces collision frequency relative to ViNT, NoMaD, and their CARE-augmented variants while maintaining goal-reaching performance.

</details>


### [126] [Improving Human Diving Endurance with a Field-Deployable, Untethered Exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11704)
*Zhihao Zhou,Zhenmeng Ju,Rui Yang,Chenxi Zhang,Zhihao Zhou,Ming Xu,Enhao Zheng,Dongjie Jiang,Lecheng Ruan,Jingeng Mai,Qining Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiveMate是一款可在真实水下环境中部署的无缆外骨骼，通过自适应踢腿辅助将人类潜水效率提升42.9%的行程距离和54.9%的持续时间，同时降低47.0%的净气体消耗率和肌肉激活水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下运动中人类耐力受限于高能耗和有限的呼吸气体供应，而外骨骼技术虽已用于陆地运动减耗，但在水下环境中增强人类耐力的潜力尚未被探索。

Method: 设计并制造了DiveMate无缆外骨骼，集成自适应踢腿辅助算法，在真实水下环境中进行自然潜水实验，测量气体消耗、肌肉激活、运动学特征等指标。

Result: 气体消耗率降低47.0%，行程距离提升42.9%，潜水时长延长54.9%，肌肉激活显著减少，运动规律性改善。

Conclusion: 外骨骼辅助技术可有效提升人类水下耐力与探索能力，拓展了外骨骼应用边界，为未来水下辅助设备的设计评估提供参考。

Abstract: Human endurance in underwater locomotion is fundamentally restricted by high energetic demands to overcome drag and the finite supply of self-contained breathing gas. While exoskeleton technology can reduce the metabolic cost of humans in terrestrial locomotion, its potential to enhance human endurance during underwater diving remains entirely unexplored. Here, we present DiveMate, a field-deployable, untethered exoskeleton designed to improve human diving endurance via adaptive kick assistance in real-world underwater environments. During naturalistic diving, DiveMate increases the travel distance using a given energy (breathing gas) by 42.9% and extends dive duration by 54.9% through reducing gas consumption rate. Marked reductions in muscle activation indicate a decrease in physiological exertion, with the net gas consumption rate decreasing by 47.0%. Kinematic characteristics and regularity improvements further underpin efficient energy economy. These results suggest that applying exoskeleton assistance is beneficial for improving human diving endurance and augmenting their ability to explore the aquatic world. This study extends the application frontier of exoskeletons and provides a potential reference for the design and assessment of future underwater assistive devices.

</details>


### [127] [Explore From Sketch: Accelerating UAV Exploration in Large-scale Environments with Prior Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11708)
*Tiancheng Lai,Yuman Gao,Xiangyu Li,Ruitian Pang,Xingpeng Wang,Siqi Shen,Mengke Zhang,Yin He,Fei Gao,Chao Xu,Yanjun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种利用不精确2D先验地图引导无人机自主探索的框架，通过鲁棒的2D-3D点云配准和分层视点规划，在大型复杂环境中提升探索效率34.2%，减少飞行距离37.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型复杂地形中无人机自主探索常因调度次优和绕路而效率低下，而现成的先验地图（如施工图）虽不精确但能提供全局结构先验，作者希望利用这种不完美先验来提升探索效率。

Method: 提出包含两部分的框架：(1) 鲁棒的2D-3D点云配准流水线（GeoContext单帧候选检索 + 多帧验证粗配准 + Scale-ICP精化），可处理地图不一致并提供多假设；(2) 分层视点规划策略（先验引导点附着 + MCTS遍历排序 + 风险感知选择器 + 固定端点TSP局部覆盖路径生成）。

Result: 基准测试相比SOTA提升探索效率34.2%，减少飞行距离37.9%；仿真与实物实验验证了对先验地图不完整和变形的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 稀疏、未对齐甚至存在偏差的2D先验地图可以有效提升LiDAR无人机探索效率，所提配准和规划框架对地图缺陷具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration with UAVs in large-scale, topologically complex environments often suffers from low efficiency due to suboptimal scheduling and detours. Prior maps (e.g., construction drawings), although usually imprecise and flawed, are readily available in many scenarios and have the potential to provide global structural guidance. This paper presents a novel exploration framework that leverages sparse, unaligned, and even discrepant 2D prior maps for LiDAR-based UAV exploration. First, a robust 2D-3D point cloud registration pipeline is proposed to align LiDAR observations with prior maps. The registration pipeline combines a GeoContext descriptor for single-frame candidate retrieval, a multi-frame verification mechanism for coarse transformation estimation with outlier rejection, and a Scale-ICP algorithm for refinement. The registration module can handle map discrepancies and provide multiple hypotheses when geometric ambiguities arise. To effectively utilize the registration results for exploration planning, we further develop a hierarchical viewpoint planning strategy under localization uncertainties. The hierarchical strategy first spatially attaches local viewpoints to prior guidepoints and adopts a Monte Carlo Tree Search solver to determine their traversal sequence under each registration hypothesis. To mitigate registration uncertainty, a risk-aware selector evaluates prior sequences using confidence-weighted travel risk, and a fixed-endpoint traveling salesman problem is formulated to generate an efficient local coverage path under the selected prior guidance. Benchmark evaluations reveal up to 34.2% improvement in exploration efficiency and 37.9% reduction in flight distance compared to state-of-the-art methods, while extensive simulations and field experiments further demonstrate robustness to prior map incompleteness and deformations.

</details>


### [128] [TacCoRL: Integrating Tactile Feedback into VLA via Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11743)
*Siyu Ma,Yuqi Liang,Chang Yu,Yunuo Chen,Hao Su,Yixin Zhu,Yin Yang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacCoRL将触觉反馈注入VLA策略，通过仿真-真实协同训练和强化学习提升接触丰富任务的操控性能，无需大规模触觉预训练或大量真实接触探索，在四项双手接触任务中平均成功率从50.0%提升至72.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操控中提供了视觉、语言和动作先验，但纯视觉观测在接触丰富任务中难以获取局部接触状态，导致任务失败。现有方法依赖大规模触觉预训练或大量真实接触探索，成本高且风险大。

Method: 提出TacCoRL框架：(1) 使用真实对齐的仿真器作为闭环接触交互训练环境；(2) 混合仿真与真实轨迹对预训练策略进行触觉条件动作的暖启动；(3) 基于可验证任务奖励的强化学习优化策略，利用仿真接触rollouts；(4) 监督目标保持策略锚定于真实部署分布。

Result: 在四项双手接触丰富任务中，最终视觉-触觉策略平均成功率72.5%，基线为50.0%。策略可直接迁移至真实机器人，无需特权仿真状态或在线真实世界强化学习。

Conclusion: TacCoRL证明了通过仿真-真实协同训练和RL，可以在不依赖大规模触觉预训练或大量真实探索的情况下，有效将触觉反馈注入VLA策略，显著提升接触丰富任务的操控性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide strong visual, language, and action priors for robot manipulation, but visual observations alone often miss the local contact state required for contact-rich tasks. We present TacCoRL, a scalable framework that injects Tactile feedback into VLA policies and improves them through sim-real Co-training and simulation-based reinforcement learning (RL), without requiring large-scale tactile pretraining or extensive real-world contact exploration. The key idea is not only adding touch as an input, but learning how contact readings should modulate action responses in near-failure states that are rare in demonstrations and risky to collect on hardware. We use a real-aligned simulator as a closed-loop training environment for contact interaction. Mixed simulated and real trajectories first warm-start tactile-conditioned actions in the pretrained policy. Reinforcement learning with verifiable task rewards then optimizes the policy using simulated contact rollouts. It reinforces tactile-conditioned actions that lead to task completion, while a supervised objective on real trajectories keeps the refined policy anchored to deployment visual, tactile, and action distributions. The resulting policy transfers directly to the real robot without privileged simulation state or online real-world RL. Across four bimanual contact-rich tasks, the final visuo-tactile policy achieves an average success rate of 72.5%, compared to baseline of 50.0%. Result videos and more details are available at https://tac-corl.github.io/

</details>


### [129] [Blind Dexterous Grasping via Real2Sim2Real Tactile Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11767)
*Shengcheng Luo,Xiyan Huang,Zhe Xu,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一套结合Real2Sim触觉校准、布局感知触觉编码器和触觉条件扩散策略的框架，实现了真实多指灵巧手仅凭触觉的盲抓取，在10个已知和10个未知物体上取得27%真实世界抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉策略从仿真迁移到真实机器人存在触觉sim-to-real差距，且稀疏触觉信号表达能力有限，导致仅有触觉的盲抓取难以在真实灵巧手上部署。

Method: 1) Real2Sim触觉校准流水线，构建能复现真实触觉信号的数字孪生仿真器；2) 布局感知触觉编码器，通过自监督预训练融入传感器几何先验；3) 在校准仿真器中训练物体特定强化学习专家，聚合成功轨迹得到触觉条件扩散策略。

Result: 在配备分布式触觉传感器的LEAP Hand上，10个已知和10个未知物体真实抓取成功率为27%；消融实验证明布局感知触觉预训练和Real2Sim校准的有效性。

Conclusion: 接触事件校准、几何感知触觉表征学习和扩散策略聚合为实现真实灵巧手仅有触觉的盲抓取提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Blind grasping with a dexterous hand is a crucial manipulation capability. Nevertheless, learning such tactile-only policies for real robots remains challenging due to the tactile sim-to-real gap and the limited expressiveness of sparse tactile signals. To bridge this gap, we propose a framework for tactile-only blind grasping that is deployable on a physical multi-fingered robotic hand. Our approach combines three key components. First, we introduce a Real2Sim tactile calibration pipeline that constructs a contact-calibrated digital-twin simulator capable of reproducing real tactile signals. Second, we improve the expressiveness of sparse tactile observations using a layout-aware tactile encoder, which incorporates sensor-geometry priors through self-supervised pretraining. Third, to improve generalization to unseen objects, we train object-specific reinforcement-learning experts in the calibrated simulator and aggregate their successful grasp trajectories into a tactile-conditioned Diffusion Policy. We evaluate our method on a physical LEAP Hand equipped with distributed tactile sensing across 10 seen and 10 unseen objects. The deployed policy achieves a 27\% real-world grasp success rate across all 20 objects, without real-world grasping demonstrations or visual input. Simulation ablations show that layout-aware tactile pretraining improves grasping performance, while sensing-level evaluations confirm that Real2Sim calibration increases the consistency of tactile contact events between simulation and hardware. Together, these results suggest that contact-event calibration, geometry-aware tactile representation learning, and diffusion-based policy aggregation provide an effective path toward tactile-only blind grasping on real dexterous robotic hands. Project page:Dex-Blind-Grasp.github.io.

</details>


### [130] [Human-Guided Co-Manipulation of Carbon Fiber Plies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11818)
*Rami Ojanen,James Fant-Male,Roel Pieters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了碳纤维铺层协作操控中不同控制方法的优缺点，提出结合语音指令、视觉手腕跟踪与力/柔顺控制的多模态方案是最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性材料自动化处理困难，纯人工操作费力低效，因此需要人机协作提升效率，而有效的控制方法是实现高效协作的关键。

Method: 在受控环境下比较了多种控制方法（语音指令、视觉手腕跟踪、力/柔顺控制等），并提出多模态组合方案。

Result: 不同控制方法各有优劣，多模态组合（语音+视觉+力/柔顺控制）在任务完整性和直观性上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 多模态控制方法是实现碳纤维铺层高效人机协作操控的最佳路径。

Abstract: The handling of flexible materials is a difficult task to fully automate due to the challenges caused by the deformability of these types of objects. Meanwhile, a fully manual process can be ergonomically challenging, tedious and inefficient. Thus, human-robot collaboration (HRC) and cooperative manipulation (co-manipulation) have received increasing interest in this field as they enable human involvement when needed while also improving productivity. To enable efficient co-manipulation and interaction between the human operator and the robot, different modalities and control methods are required. In this paper, we present and examine different control methods for co-manipulation of carbon fiber plies, evaluating the pros and cons of each method in a controlled setting. We propose that a multimodal combination of speech commands, wrist-tracking through vision, and force with compliant control would provide the best solution for complete and intuitive control of the task.

</details>


### [131] [Modular Anthropomorphic Hand Design via Multi-Parameter Finger Benchmarking and Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11826)
*Yu Zhang,Huijiang Wang,Josie Hughes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种模块化手指优化框架，通过独立优化手指设计（关节、骨骼、皮肤、传感器等）并量化评估，再集成到完整遥操作手部，提升拟人机器人手部整体灵巧操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化方法缺乏结构或仅考虑单一指标，难以系统比较和针对性改进；手指特性对灵巧性至关重要但未被充分独立优化。

Method: 开发模块化集成平台，将不同手指原型（变体包括关节、骨骼、皮肤、传感器位置）通过机制导向和任务相关指标定量评估，筛选后集成到完整手部进行任务级验证。

Result: 通过多物体抓取和灯泡拧动等任务验证，优化后的手指显著提升了整体手部性能。

Conclusion: 模块化手指优化与定量评估框架能有效桥接组件设计与整体手部性能，为拟人灵巧手设计提供系统化方法。

Abstract: Designing anthropomorphic dexterous robotic hands remains challenging as the design space straddles morphology, actuation, and sensing properties, and performance metrics span both task-dependent and task-agnostic. Existing optimization methods are often unstructured or consider only a single performance metric, limiting systematic comparison and targeted refinement. While the design considerations of the entire hand are significant, the individual finger properties play a key role in dexterity. By developing a robotic hand platform where fingers can be modularly integrated into a full teleoperated hand, we propose that optimizing the fingers can significantly improve overall hand performance. This approach enables rapid screening of different finger-level prototypes through a number of quantitative benchmarks before their integration into the hand for task-level validation. Candidate finger designs (incorporating variations in joint, bone, skin, and sensor placement) are assessed using both mechanism-oriented and task-relevant metrics, which establish a quantitative link between component design and full hand embodiment. The framework is validated through the development of an anthropomorphic robotic hand with optimized fingers, demonstrating how these fingers enable performance improvements across tasks, including multi-object grasping and light bulb screwing.

</details>


### [132] [Critic Architecture Matters: Dual vs. Unified Critics for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11891)
*Mehmet Turan Yardımcı*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在人形机器人多目标强化学习中，双评论家架构（分离价值函数）相比统一评论家架构在操作效率上提升显著（到达速度3.5倍、吞吐量2倍、成功率65.2% vs 53.8%），且奖励工程改造对此无额外增益，表明评论家架构是比奖励设计更关键的决策因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标强化学习下的人形机器人需要同时协调移动与操作，但评论家架构（统一vs分离）是否影响多目标权衡一直缺乏系统对比验证。

Method: 在NVIDIA Isaac Lab中基于Unitree G1（23自由度）训练移动操作策略，采用13级顺序课程（从静止抓取到变位姿行走），对比统一评论家与双评论家架构，并额外测试反博弈奖励机制。

Result: 双评论家策略到达速度快3.5倍（6.5 vs 22.6步），吞吐量2倍（14.3 vs 7.0次/千步），成功率65.2% vs 53.8%；反博弈奖励机制未带来额外提升（60.9% vs 65.2%）。

Conclusion: 评论家架构是多目标人形机器人RL中被忽视的关键设计选择，其影响远超奖励工程；统一评论家在微调预训练策略时可能因竞争梯度抑制已学行为。

Abstract: Multi-objective reinforcement learning for humanoid robots must coordinate locomotion and manipulation within a single policy. A natural design choice is whether to use a single (unified) critic that estimates the combined value of all objectives, or separate (dual) critics with disjoint reward signals. We present a controlled comparison on the Unitree G1 humanoid (23 active DoF) in NVIDIA Isaac Lab, training loco-manipulation policies through a sequential curriculum spanning 13 levels from stationary reaching to walking with variable-orientation targets. In standardized evaluation, dual-critic policies reach targets 3.5$\times$ faster (6.5 vs. 22.6 simulation steps), achieve 2$\times$ higher throughput (14.3 vs. 7.0 validated reaches per 1,000 steps), and attain higher validated reach rates (65.2% vs. 53.8%) compared to the unified-critic policy. Notably, additional anti-gaming reward mechanisms provide no further improvement beyond the architectural change alone (60.9% vs. 65.2%). These results have direct implications for the emerging paradigm of RL fine-tuning of imitation-learned policies: when refining a pre-trained manipulation policy with RL, a unified critic risks suppressing the learned behavior through competing locomotion gradients. These findings demonstrate that critic architecture is a primary - and often overlooked - design choice in multi-objective humanoid RL, with greater impact than reward engineering on reaching efficiency.

</details>


### [133] [DuoBench: A Reproducible Benchmark for Bimanual Manipulation in Simulation and the Real World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11901)
*Tobias Jülg,Seongjin Bien,Simon Hilber,Yannik Blei,Pierre Krack,Maximilian Li,Sven Parusel,Rudolf Lioutikov,Florian Walter,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DuoBench 是一个面向 FR3 Duo 双臂机器人平台的可扩展基准测试框架，包含 11 个任务（覆盖 4 种协调类型），支持仿真和真实世界评估，采用阶段式评价方案进行细粒度语义失败分析，并提供了人类遥操作数据集。实验表明当前策略在双臂操作中仍面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法很好地捕捉双臂操作中双臂协调带来的额外控制复杂度和失败模式，需要系统化、可复现的测试平台来诊断这些失败模式并推动方法研究。

Method: 提出 DuoBench 框架：11 个任务覆盖 4 种协调类别；阶段式评价方案（分阶段检测失败点，超越二值成功/失败）；仿真+真实世界复现（含 3D 打印资产）；提供人类遥操作数据集；在多个双臂模仿学习和 VLA 策略上进行基准测试。

Result: 当前策略在双臂操作中整体表现不佳，尤其在早期交互阶段、并行双臂执行以及 sim-to-real 迁移方面存在显著困难。

Conclusion: DuoBench 为双臂操作策略的失败模式诊断和未来方法研究提供了可复现的测试平台，双臂操作仍是开放挑战。

Abstract: Bimanual robot systems substantially expand manipulation capabilities, but coordinating two arms introduces additional control complexity and failure modes that are not well captured by existing benchmarks. We introduce DuoBench, an extensible benchmarking framework for bimanual manipulation policies on the FR3 Duo platform. DuoBench comprises eleven tasks spanning four coordination categories, implemented in simulation and partially reproduced in the real world through reproducible task recipes with 3D-printable assets. In addition, we propose a stage-based evaluation scheme that supports fine-grained semantic failure analysis beyond binary success and provide human-teleoperated datasets for all benchmark tasks. We benchmark several dual-arm imitation-learning and vision-language-action policies in simulation and on real hardware. Our results show that current policies remain challenged by bimanual manipulation, particularly in early interaction stages, parallel arm execution, and transfer between simulation and real-world settings. DuoBench provides a reproducible testbed for diagnosing these failure modes and studying future methods for dual-arm policy learning. Code, datasets, and videos are available at https://duobench.github.io/

</details>


### [134] [Deformable In-Hand Slip-Aware Tactile Sensor with Integrated Velocity, Force/Torque, and Pressure Map Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11952)
*Gabriel Arslan Waltersson,Yiannis Karayiannidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型触觉传感器，将速度、力/力矩和压力图传感集成到一个带有可变形接触垫的单一器件中，用于带滑觉感知的灵巧手内操作。这是首个在单一柔性结构中组合这些感知模态的传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉传感器通常只能提供单一的传感模态（如仅压力或仅力/力矩），无法满足灵巧操作中同时需要滑觉检测、力控和接触分布感知的需求。本文旨在通过多模态融合设计提升机器人手内操作能力。

Method: 采用标准PCB制造与快速原型技术结合的方案，设计一个可变形接触垫结构，集成速度（滑觉）、六维力/力矩和压力分布三种传感模态于单一柔性器件中。

Result: 通过全面的实验评估，传感器能够鲁棒地跟踪平面和曲面，适用于多种材料，验证了其多模态感知能力与设计可行性。

Conclusion: 该传感器实现了低成本快速制造的多模态触觉传感，为灵巧操作中的滑觉感知与力控提供了硬件基础，但实验部分主要展示能力与局限，未提供与SOTA的量化对比或公开代码。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel tactile sensor for in-hand manipulation with slip-aware control that integrates velocity, force/torque, and pressure map sensing into a single device with a deformable contact pad. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first sensor to combine these sensing modalities within a single compliant structure. The sensor features a deformable contact surface and can robustly track both flat and curved surfaces across a wide range of materials. Its performance is evaluated through a comprehensive set of experiments that highlight both its capabilities and limitations. The sensor is designed for rapid and low-cost fabrication using a combination of standard PCB manufacturing and rapid prototyping techniques.

</details>


### [135] [MPPI-based Informative Trajectory Planning for Search and Capture of Drifting Targets with ASVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12019)
*Sanjeev Ramkumar Sudha,Marija Popović,Erlend M. Coates*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于MPPI控制器的混合规划框架，用于ASV在动态环境中搜索并捕获多个漂移目标（如垃圾），通过长时域轨迹优化平衡搜索与跟踪，并在拦截阶段切换到纯追踪制导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标跟踪与追击方法多采用简单制导行为和短时预测，难以有效平衡未知区域的探索与已知目标的跟踪，尤其是在动态环境中处理持续漂移的多个目标。

Method: 采用混合规划框架：基于模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制的时空信息性规划方法，通过采样优化连续轨迹，结合多目标代价函数平衡搜索与跟踪；拦截阶段切换至纯追踪制导控制器。

Result: 实验表明该规划器优于所选规划基线，并在ASV实地试验中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提混合框架能有效解决动态环境中多漂移目标的搜索与捕获问题，长时域MPPI规划与纯追踪制导的组合实用有效。

Abstract: Autonomous surface vehicles offer an efficient solution for environmental cleanup as well as search and rescue operations in open waters. Targets in these settings drift continuously, so efficient search must balance exploration of unobserved regions with tracking of known targets. However, most target tracking and pursuit scenarios consider simple guidance behaviours and short-term predictions for decision-making. In this letter, we address the problem of search and capture of multiple drifting targets, such as litter, in dynamic environments, using a hybrid planning framework. A key aspect of our strategy is a spatiotemporal informative planning method based on model predictive path integral (MPPI) control, a sampling-based model predictive control approach. The planner directly generates kinematic-level commands by optimising continuous trajectories over long horizons. A multi-objective cost balances search and tracking objectives while ensuring safe, feasible trajectories. In the interception stage, we switch to a pure pursuit guidance controller for the physical capture of moving targets. Experiments show that our planner outperforms the chosen planning baselines. Finally, we validate our approach in field trials with an ASV.

</details>


### [136] [Learning Unions of Convex Sets via Invertible Latent Decomposition for Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12027)
*Taerim Yoon,Dongho Kang,Kisang Park,Junha Cha,Stelian Coros,Sungjoon Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ILD（可逆潜分解）框架，联合学习可逆映射和隐空间中的凸多面体并集，实现碰撞自由路径规划。通过可逆映射将隐空间中的凸集规划路径解码回原始配置空间，同时保持可行性。提出可见性引导采样（VGS）保持凸集连通性。在2D导航、6-DoF和14-DoF操作环境中，相比基线方法获得更广的覆盖、更好的连通性和更高的路径规划成功率，零假阳性，并在14-DoF双臂机械臂上实现实时规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有碰撞空间表示分两类：显式表示（凸集并集）可嵌入优化规划器作为硬约束但参数随维度扩展性差；隐式表示灵活可扩展但缺乏保障。希望桥接二者，同时获得显式的凸集约束和隐式的可扩展性。

Method: 1) 联合学习一个可逆映射（从原始C空间到隐空间）和隐空间中的凸多面体并集；2) 在隐空间凸集上进行路径规划，通过可逆映射解码回原始空间并保证可行性；3) 提出可见性引导采样（VGS）保持凸集间连通性，支持路径规划。

Result: 在2D导航、6-DoF和14-DoF操作环境中，相比基线方法：更广的覆盖面积，更好的集间连通性，更高的路径规划成功率。测试后精炼后零假阳性。在14-DoF双臂机械臂上实现实时碰撞自由规划，并能适应真实世界部署中场景几何变化。

Conclusion: ILD成功桥接了显式和隐式碰撞空间表示的鸿沟，在保持显式凸集约束可优化性的同时，通过可逆映射实现隐式表示的灵活性和可扩展性，是机器人运动规划领域的一项重要进展。

Abstract: Collision-free path planning in cluttered, real-world environments relies on a representation of the collision-free space, and existing representations broadly fall into two
  categories. Explicit representations, such as unions of convex sets, can be plugged into optimization-based planners as hard collision-free constraints, but their parameters scale
  poorly with configuration-space dimension. Implicit representations, by contrast, are flexible and scale well to complex geometries, yet typically lack such guarantees. We bridge this
  gap with ILD (Invertible Latent Decomposition), a framework that jointly learns an invertible mapping and a union of explicit convex polytopes in the resulting latent space. Planning
  is carried out over these latent convex sets, and the invertible mapping decodes the resulting paths back to the original configuration space while preserving feasibility with
  respect to the refined explicit safe regions. We further propose Visibility-Guided Sampling (VGS) to keep the convex sets connected for path planning. Across 2D navigation, 6-DoF, and
  14-DoF manipulation environments, ILD achieves broader coverage, better inter-set connectivity, and higher path-planning success rates than prior baselines, with zero observed false
  positives after test-time refinement. On a 14-DoF bimanual manipulator, we further demonstrate real-time collision-free planning, with test-time refinement adapting to scene-geometry
  changes during real-world deployment on a single 6-DoF arm.

</details>


### [137] [VICX: Generalizable Robot Manipulation via Video Generation and In-Context Operator Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12028)
*Song Chen,Linyan Xiang,Ying Zhou,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VICX提出了一种解耦的闭环操作框架，将冻结的视频生成模型作为高层视觉规划器，结合V2T-ICON网络将视觉规划转化为机器人状态轨迹，通过分割提取的臂部观测和检索式上下文提示实现任务无关的视觉到状态映射，在Meta-World上验证了跨任务泛化、闭环自校正和跨本体迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作方法难以同时实现场景层面的任务推理和具身层面执行的可泛化落地，两者之间存在视觉规划与具体执行的鸿沟。

Method: VICX采用解耦架构：(1) 冻结的视频生成模型生成视觉语言条件的高层视觉计划；(2) V2T-ICON作为任务无关接口，利用分割提取的臂部观测和检索到的图像-状态对作为上下文提示，将视觉计划映射为可执行的机器人状态轨迹，无需参数更新。

Result: 在Meta-World基准上验证了VICX支持跨任务泛化、闭环自校正和跨本体迁移，实现了任务语义和机器人执行的双重泛化。

Conclusion: 解耦的视频生成+上下文执行框架能有效弥合视觉规划与具身执行之间的差距，实现跨任务、跨本体的可泛化机器人操作。

Abstract: Generalizable robot manipulation requires not only task-level reasoning over unseen scenes, but also reliable grounding of visual plans into embodiment-specific execution. To bridge this gap, we propose VICX (Video generation and In-Context eXecution), a decoupled closed-loop manipulation framework. In VICX, a frozen video generation model produces vision-language-conditioned high-level visual plans, while a Video-to-Trajectory In-Context Operator Network (V2T-ICON) serves as the task-agnostic interface that grounds these plans into executable robot-state trajectories. To improve execution generalization, V2T-ICON operates on segmentation-extracted arm-only frame observations and uses retrieved image-state pairs as in-context prompts, allowing a robust and generalizable visual-to-state mapping at inference time without parameter updates. Experiments on Meta-World show that VICX supports cross-task generalization, closed-loop self-correction, and cross-embodiment transfer, demonstrating dual generalization across both task semantics and robot execution. The project webpage can be found here: https://scaling-group.github.io/vicx/.

</details>


### [138] [KinematicRL: A Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning Framework For Social Navigation With Kinodynamic Feasibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12042)
*Zhiming Xu,Haodong Yang,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen,Chenpeng Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出KinematicRL统一框架，通过二阶控制动作空间、基于iLQR的预训练、仅2D LiDAR的聚类人体追踪以及无偏残差门控模块，解决社交导航中sim-to-real差距，实现差分驱动机器人真实世界部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRL社交导航方法因简化的一阶动力学和特定场景的人体状态估计，存在严重的sim-to-real差距，阻碍真实世界部署。

Method: 1) 理论证明高阶控制可指数级降低跟踪误差，提出二阶差分驱动机器人控制动作空间；2) 用随机iLQR通过散度最小化预训练策略；3) 仅用2D LiDAR的聚类追踪管线，结合空间邻近和速度相似性进行关联；4) 无偏残差门控模块平衡反应与记忆行为，处理时变人群规模。

Result: KinematicRL在真实世界差分驱动机器人上部署，仅需最小修改即可运行，持续改善运动学性能并适应不同检测人数。

Conclusion: 统一框架有效弥合sim-to-real差距，二阶控制+高效追踪+残差门控的组合使DRL社交导航策略可实际部署。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) has shown promise for social navigation, yet its real-world deployment remains hindered by a persistent sim-to-real gap arising from simplified first-order dynamics and context-specific human state estimation pipelines. This work presents a unified framework that addresses these limitations to produce dynamically feasible navigation policies suitable for real-world deployment. First, theoretical analysis reveals that tracking error between simulated and actual robot position decays exponentially with increased control order, motivating the use of higher-order control inputs as DRL action space. A second-order control formulation tailored to differential drive robots is developed, complemented by a stochastic iterative Linear Quadratic Regulator (iLQR) that pretrains the policy via a divergence minimization objective. Second, to avoid the added system complexity of camera-LiDAR fusion, a cluster-based human tracking pipeline using only 2D LiDAR is introduced. Human detections are associated according to both spatial proximity and velocity similarity, enabling reliable differentiation of nearby pedestrians and yielding stable velocity estimates through temporal aggregation. Third, we introduce an unbiased residual gating block to balance reaction- and memory-based behaviors while handling time-varying crowd sizes, both critical for social navigation. The resulting policy, KinematicRL, consistently improves kinematic performance and adapts to varying number of detected humans. Experiments in real-world environments demonstrate that, when combined with the proposed tracking pipeline, KinematicRL can be deployed on a real differential drive robot with minimal modifications.

</details>


### [139] [Point Cloud Segmentation for Autonomous Clip Positioning in Laparoscopic Cholecystectomy on a Phantom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12048)
*Balázs Gyenes,Nikolai Franke,Paul Maria Scheikl,Pit Henrich,Rayan Younis,Gerhard Neumann,Martin Wagner,Franziska Mathis-Ullrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首个在腹腔镜手术中实现自主夹子定位的机器人系统，使用合成点云预训练+数据增强克服数据稀缺，在物理体模上达到0.75mm精度和100%执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度机器人手术系统需要兼具精确性和可解释性，且手术领域数据稀缺，现有系统难以满足低容错环境下的自主操作需求。

Method: 单相机无色点云分割 → 样条插值提取夹子目标位置 → 人机交互调整 → 运动规划可视化。分割模型使用12.8万合成点云预训练 + 60个真实点云微调 + 两种新型数据增强。

Result: 目标定位精度0.75mm (95%成功率)，自主夹子放置100%成功率，运动可视化满足微创手术约束。

Conclusion: 证明了合成数据+少量真实数据可解决手术机器人数据稀缺问题，方法可推广到其他需要精确定位与导航的手术/非手术任务。

Abstract: High-risk applications in robotics, such as robot-assisted surgery, present unique challenges. These systems must be both highly precise and interpretable in order to be deployed in environments with very low tolerance for error or unsafe exploration. We present the first robotic system to demonstrate autonomous clip positioning on a physical phantom in laparoscopic surgery, one of the most common interventions in general surgery. After segmentation of a colorless point cloud from a single camera, target positions for the clips are extracted using spline interpolation, and can then be adjusted by the human operator. The segmentation model is trained on only 60 hand-labeled real point clouds, reflecting data scarcity in the surgical domain. We overcome this with a combination of pre-training on 128,000 synthetic point clouds and two novel data augmentation techniques. The motion of the end-effector to each target is visualized for the operator, satisfying the unique motion constraints of minimally-invasive surgery while ensuring that the robot's actions are verifiable and interpretable. In real robot experiments, our system localizes targets with the required precision of 0.75mm at a 95% success rate and executes autonomous clip positioning with a 100% success rate. We provide insights that are applicable to many other surgical and non-surgical tasks that require identifying and navigating to a precise target. Source code and project page: https://github.com/balazsgyenes/kirurc

</details>


### [140] [Fibration Trees: A Unified Approach to Multi-Robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12070)
*Andreas Orthey,Florian T. Pokorny,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出纤维树（fibration trees）统一框架，将优先级投影、并行分解和任务空间投影整合为一种形式化方法，并基于此开发了Fibration-RRT规划器，在高达96自由度的多机器人场景中验证了其概率完备性和高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有状态空间投影与分解方法缺乏统一框架，无法无缝处理优先级投影、并行分解和任务空间投影的组合，导致高维多机器人运动规划中缺乏通用的形式化工具。

Method: 提出纤维树（fibration trees），以状态空间为节点、纤维（fibration）为边，将投影建模为纤维；在此基础上开发Fibration-RRT算法，统一了商空间RRT（顺序优先级）和离散RRT（并行分解）的策略，并支持任务空间投影，且证明了概率完备性。

Result: 在32个多机器人场景（最高96自由度）上进行实验，Fibration-RRT能够高效求解高维问题，公开了开源实现。

Conclusion: 纤维树为多机器人运动规划提供了统一且强大的框架，Fibration-RRT通过利用用户定义的纤维树高效解决高维问题。

Abstract: State space projections and decompositions have emerged as powerful tools to tackle the curse of dimensionality in high-dimensional, multi-robot motion planning problems. However, existing methods lack a unified framework which seamlessly handles combinations of projections (prioritization or task-space) and decompositions (parallel or decoupled subspaces). To fill this gap, we introduce fibration trees, which are trees consisting of state spaces as nodes and fibrations as edges, whereby a fibration models a projection from a higher-dimensional space to a lower-dimensional (or simplified) space. By modeling projections as fibrations, we unify sequential prioritization, parallel decomposition, and task-space projections under a single, coherent formalism. Building on this, we develop the rapidly-exploring random fibration trees (Fibration-RRT) planner, a sampling-based motion planner that generalizes strategies from quotient-space RRT (for sequential prioritizations) and discrete RRT (for parallel decompositions), while allowing the inclusion of task-space projections. Fibration-RRT operates on user-defined fibration trees and is proven to be probabilistically complete. To test the generality and efficiency of Fibration-RRT, we provide an open-source implementation and conduct experiments on 32 scenarios using multi robot teams with up to 96 degrees of freedom. Our results indicate that Fibration-RRT efficiently solves high-dimensional problems by exploiting user-defined fibration trees, thereby establishing fibration trees as a powerful, unified framework for multi-robot motion planning.

</details>


### [141] [DAM-VLA: Decoupled Asynchronous Multimodal Vision Language Action model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12105)
*Pankhuri Vanjani,Zhuoyue Li,Jakub Suliga,Moritz Reuss,Gianluca Geraci,Xinkai Jiang,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DAM-VLA，通过为每个模态（触觉、视觉、语言）解耦时间处理，维护按传感器频率更新的模态潜变量缓冲区，并通过门控交叉注意力将高频新模态集成到预训练骨干网络中，实现高频（100Hz）、平滑、鲁棒的机器人操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型继承视觉-语言预训练的同步时钟，以单一速率处理所有输入，与物理交互中不同模态（高速触觉、中速视觉、恒定语言）差异巨大的更新频率不匹配，导致慢模态过采样、快模态欠采样，动作生成受限于最低有效频率。

Method: 为每个模态维护潜变量缓冲区，以各自传感器频率刷新，动作头连续读取；通过门控交叉注意力将新高频模态（如触觉）集成到预训练骨干中，保持骨干网络完整。

Result: 在7个接触丰富的真实世界操控任务中，平均成功率比最强同步基线提升超过一倍（95.2% vs 40.95%），同时维持100Hz平滑反应式控制。

Conclusion: 解耦模态时间处理能产生更强的表征和更鲁棒的控制，为VLA模型在物理交互中的高效部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models inherit a shared synchronous clock from vision-language pretraining, processing every input at one rate. This is misaligned with physical interaction, where a high-frequency modality changes at hundreds of hertz, vision evolves more slowly, and language stays constant across an episode. A synchronous VLA oversamples slow modalities, undersamples fast ones, and caps action generation at the lowest effective frequency. We hypothesize that decoupling temporal processing per modality, letting each update and retain information at its own sensor rate, yields stronger representations and more robust control. We present DAM-VLA, which maintains per-modality latent buffers refreshed at sensor rates and read continuously by the action head, integrating new high-frequency modalities through gated cross-attention that leaves the pretrained backbone intact. Across seven contact-rich real-world manipulation tasks, DAM-VLA more than doubles the average success rate of the strongest synchronous baseline (95.2\% vs.\ 40.95\%) while sustaining smooth, reactive 100\,Hz control. Project website: \href{https://intuitive-robots.github.io/DAM-VLA/}{intuitive-robots.github.io/DAM-VLA/}

</details>


### [142] [Bridging the Morphology Gap: Adapting VLA Models to Dexterous Manipulation via Intent-Conditioned Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12109)
*Chuanke Pang,Junyi Huang,Zhijun Zhao,Yaobing Wang,Kun Xu,Xilun Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出InDex框架，通过跨形态语义继承将预训练VLA模型从低自由度平行夹爪适配到高自由度灵巧手，采用两阶段解耦学习（阶段一高效微调VLA预测臂轨迹和抓取意图标量，阶段二冻结空间主干并用意图条件扩散头解码手指关节动作），在多种灵巧操作任务中以极少量演示数据显著优于端到端微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型几乎全部局限于低自由度平行夹爪，直接将其微调到高自由度灵巧手存在严重的形态差距、灾难性遗忘和数据稀疏导致的动作流形坍塌问题，亟需一种数据高效的跨形态适应方法。

Method: 两阶段解耦学习：(1) 参数高效微调VLA骨干，输出连续臂轨迹和标量抓取意图；(2) 冻结空间骨干，使用意图条件去噪扩散头解码多指手指关节动作。核心思想是将预训练的1-DoF抓取输出重新用作连续宏观虚拟抓取意图代理。

Result: 在一系列多阶段、富接触灵巧操作仿真基准中，InDex以极少量演示数据有效掌握复杂技能，显著优于整体微调基线，同时保持原VLA先验的鲁棒空间泛化能力。

Conclusion: 跨形态语义继承+两阶段解耦范式是一种数据高效、可保留预训练空间推理能力的灵巧手VLA适应方案，为克服形态鸿沟提供了新路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable zero-shot generalization in robotic manipulation, yet the vast majority of pre-trained pipelines remain strictly confined to low-DoF parallel grippers. Adapting these rich semantic priors to high-DoF dexterous hands introduces a severe morphology gap, direct end-to-end joint fine-tuning inherently causes catastrophic forgetting of spatial reasoning and acute action manifold collapse due to data scarcity. In this paper, we present InDex, a novel, data-efficient adaptation framework rooted in cross-morphology semantic inheritance. Rather than discarding the pre-trained 1-DoF parallel grasp output, we repurpose it as a continuous, macroscopic virtual grasp intent proxy to sequentialize the control topology. We implement a two-stage decoupled learning architecture: the first stage parameter-efficiently aligns the VLA backbone to predict continuous arm trajectories and the scalar grasp intent; the second stage freezes this spatial backbone and leverages an intent-conditioned denoising diffusion head to decode fine-grained joint articulations for multi-fingered end-effectors. Extensive simulation benchmarks across a suite of multi-stage, contact-rich dexterous manipulation tasks demonstrate that InDex effectively masters intricate skills with minimal demonstration data, substantially outperforming monolithic baselines while preserving the robust spatial generalizability of the original VLA prior.

</details>


### [143] [PEBRE: An Open-Hardware Compute and Perception Add-On for the Pepper Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12112)
*Malte Kuhlmann,Ignacio Bugueno-Cordova,Emil Alms,Javier Ruiz-del-Solar,Nicolás Navarro-Guerrero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计了PEBRE——一个面向Pepper机器人的开源硬件扩展模块，集成Jetson Orin Nano、RealSense D435i等外部组件，显著提升其计算与感知能力，并保持该平台在生命周期外持续可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pepper机器人计算与感知能力有限，且处于生命周期末期，需要低成本、开源的硬件扩展方案以维持其作为研究平台的可用性，并加速软件开发和组件集成。

Method: 设计并实现了一个开源硬件扩展模块PEBRE，集成Jetson Orin Nano（计算）、Intel RealSense D435i（深度视觉）、Logitech BRIO（高清摄像头）、Samson UB1和RØDE VideoMicro II（音频）等外部组件，作为Pepper的附加模块。

Result: 实验验证表明，新硬件显著提升了Pepper的感知能力和计算性能，使该平台在生命周期外仍具备有效的研究用途。

Conclusion: PEBRE作为开源硬件和软件模块化扩展，有效延长了Pepper机器人的研究使用寿命，为社区提供了可复用的硬件加速方案。

Abstract: This paper presents the design, development, and experimental verification of PEBRE, an open-hardware add-on for fast software development on the Pepper Robot. Our project enhances Pepper's computational and perception capabilities by integrating external components such as a Jetson Orin Nano, Logitech BRIO, Intel RealSense D435i, Samson UB1, and RØDE VideoMicro II. Our results show that the new hardware considerably improved Pepper's perception abilities and computational power. This development contributes to the community by implementing an open hardware and open-source modular add-on to the Pepper robot and keeping this relevant research platform functional beyond its expected lifespan. With PEBRE, we aim to facilitate faster software development and more efficient integration of external components, ultimately enhancing the capabilities of the Pepper robot.

</details>


### [144] [AerialClaw: An Open-Source Framework for LLM-Driven Autonomous Aerial Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12142)
*Ke Li,Jianfei Yang,Luyao Zhang,Guo Yu,Chengwei Yan,Yuan Ding,Di Wang,Nan Luo,Gang Liu,Xiao Gao,Quan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AerialClaw 是一个开源框架，使无人机能够通过 LLM 智能体理解自然语言任务、调用空中技能、感知反馈并闭环迭代决策，采用模块化的脑-技能-运行时架构，支持多种仿真环境和真实部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机系统依赖预定义指令序列或特定任务管线，开发者需手动连接感知、规划、控制、仿真、日志和安全模块，缺乏灵活性、可复现性和可扩展性，限制了无人机作为自主决策智能体的能力。

Method: 提出 AerialClaw 开源框架，采用脑-技能-运行时架构：LLM 智能体作为"大脑"理解任务并维护上下文，硬技能（原子 UAV 操作）和软技能（Markdown 格式可复用任务策略）作为可执行技能，文档驱动智能体状态与能力边界，记忆驱动反思，安全导向运行时验证，平台无关执行适配器；支持轻量级模拟、PX4 SITL+Gazebo、AirSim，并提供 Web 控制台、可插拔模型后端、示例任务和部署脚本。

Result: 提出了一个可复现且可扩展的开源框架，使无人机系统能够解读任务、做出决策、执行技能并根据反馈自适应行为。论文开源实现并提供了多种仿真环境和部署支持。

Conclusion: AerialClaw 通过标准化空中技能、文档驱动智能体状态、记忆和闭环 LLM 决策，为构建可复现且可扩展的自主无人机系统提供了有效框架，将无人机从指令执行平台转变为决策智能体。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly used in inspection, search and rescue, environmental monitoring, and emergency response. However, most UAV applications still rely on pre-defined command sequences or task-specific pipelines, where developers manually connect perception, planning, flight control, simulation, logging, and safety modules. This limits the flexibility, reproducibility, and extensibility of autonomous aerial systems. This paper presents AerialClaw, an open-source software framework that enables UAVs to operate as decision-making aerial agents rather than merely command-following platforms. Given a natural-language mission, AerialClaw allows an LLM-based agent to understand the task, maintain context, invoke executable aerial skills, observe perception and runtime feedback, and iteratively update its decisions in a closed loop. The framework adopts a modular brain-skill-runtime architecture, combining hard skills for atomic UAV operations, Markdown-based soft skills for reusable task strategies, document-driven agent state and capability boundaries, memory-driven reflection, safety-oriented runtime validation, and platform-agnostic execution adapters. AerialClaw supports lightweight mock execution, PX4 SITL with Gazebo, and AirSim-based simulation, together with a web console, pluggable model backends, example missions, simulation assets, and staged deployment scripts. By combining standardized aerial skills, document-driven agent state, memory, and closed-loop LLM decision-making, AerialClaw provides a reproducible and extensible open-source framework for building UAV systems that can interpret missions, make decisions, execute skills, and adapt their behavior from feedback.

</details>


### [145] [Intelligent Automation for Embodied Benchmark Construction: Pipelines, Embodiments, Simulators, and Trends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12207)
*Jinshan Lai,Jianwei Hu,Baoyang Jiang,Fengchun Zhang,Leyuan Wang,Haotian Li,Yida Wang,Tingxuan Huang,Xi Ren,Qiang Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了具身智能基准构建的全流程（需求构建→数据采集→清洗标注→基准套件生成→评估执行），指出自动化并非简单地降低成本，而是将成本转移到验证、审计、版本控制和长期治理上。结论是具身评估的进步不仅依赖更大的基准集，更需要可诊断、可审计、可负责任更新的构建管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能的快速发展（导航、家庭辅助、操作、自动驾驶、空中智能体、多模态大模型控制等）使得基准构建成为可靠评估的核心瓶颈。传统的静态数据集无法满足具身评估需求，亟需系统性的方法论来指导基准构建。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，提出五阶段构建管线框架，系统分析从人工策展到传统自动化、基础模型辅助和智能体闭环工作流的演进，并对比了各阶段的定性与定量成本。

Result: 自动化并不单纯降低基准成本，而是将成本向验证、审计、版本控制和长期治理等方向转移。不同自动化水平在各阶段各有优劣，需要根据场景权衡。

Conclusion: 具身评估的进步不仅依赖更大的基准套件，更需要可诊断、可审计、可负责任更新的构建管线。自动化带来的成本重分配是未来具身基准构建的关键挑战。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence now spans navigation, household assistance, manipulation, autonomous driving, aerial agents, and multimodal large-model control. This expansion has made benchmark construction a central bottleneck for reliable evaluation. Unlike static datasets, embodied benchmarks combine task specifications, environments, robot data, demonstrations, annotations, metrics, evaluation scripts, and release policies into a single evaluation system. This survey reviews the literature through a five-stage construction pipeline: requirement and task construction, data acquisition, data cleaning and annotation, benchmark suite generation and metric definition, and evaluation execution with diagnostic feedback. For each stage, the survey analyzes the transition from manual curation to traditional automation, foundation-model assistance, and agentic closed-loop workflows. It also compares qualitative construction costs across human labor, data and asset acquisition, compute and simulation, validation and debugging, governance and maintenance, and rework risk. The main conclusion is that automation does not simply reduce benchmark cost. Instead, it often shifts cost toward validation, auditability, version control, and long-term governance. Progress in embodied evaluation will therefore depend not only on larger benchmark suites, but also on construction pipelines that are diagnosable, auditable, and responsibly refreshable.

</details>


### [146] [DrivingAgent: Design and Scheduling Agents for Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12236)
*Zhongyu Xia,Wenhao Chen,Yongtao Wang,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DrivingAgent，一个面向自动驾驶系统设计和实时调度的智能体框架。设计阶段自动化模块开发（理解架构→生成代码→超网络训练验证），调度阶段使用强化学习训练的轻量级LLM动态编排模块，配合结构化的长短期记忆系统。在nuScenes和Bench2Drive上取得更优的速度-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统集成基础模型时面临两大挑战：(i) 手动设计和集成新模型耗时费力，(ii) 缺乏智能的动态调度机制满足严格实时约束。现有LLM代理框架不适合自动驾驶，无法区分系统设计和实时调度的不同需求，将模块视为黑箱，且不支持持续运行。

Method: DrivingAgent包含两个阶段：(1) 设计阶段——通过解释系统架构、生成代码、超网络训练验证来自动化模块开发；(2) 调度阶段——使用强化学习训练的轻量级LLM动态编排系统模块，配合集成长期存储与带时间戳短期上下文的结构化记忆。

Result: 在nuScenes和Bench2Drive基准上取得了更优的速度-精度权衡。

Conclusion: DrivingAgent有效解决了自动驾驶中系统设计和实时调度的双重挑战，实现了自动化模块开发与智能动态调度，在实际基准测试中验证了其优势。

Abstract: Many autonomous driving systems are increasingly incorporating foundation models to improve generalization and handle long-tail scenarios. However, this trend introduces two key challenges: (i) the manual and labor-intensive process of designing and integrating new models, and (ii) the lack of intelligent, dynamic scheduling mechanisms to meet strict real-time constraints. While Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents offer a promising avenue for automation, existing frameworks are ill-suited for autonomous driving. Specifically, they fail to distinguish between the fundamentally different requirements of system design and real-time scheduling, treat modules as opaque black boxes, and are not designed for continuous operation. To address these limitations, we propose DrivingAgent, a novel agent framework tailored to the dual challenges of autonomous driving system design and scheduling. In the design phase, DrivingAgent automates module development by interpreting system architecture, generating code, and validating modules via super-network training. In the scheduling phase, it employs a lightweight LLM trained with reinforcement learning to dynamically orchestrate system modules in real time, supported by a structured memory that integrates long-term storage with timestamped short-term context. Experimental results demonstrate that DrivingAgent achieves a superior speed--accuracy trade-off on both the nuScenes and Bench2Drive benchmarks.

</details>


### [147] [Learning What to Say to Your VLA: Mostly Harmless Vision Language Action Model Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12299)
*Hyun Joe Jeong,Gokul Swamy,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个交互式搜索语言序列的框架，优化冻结的VLA模型闭环任务性能，蒸馏为测试时语言反馈策略（LFP），并添加合规化改进头以防止有害干预。在仿真中提升24.7%，硬件上提升65.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型中语言到行为的映射脆弱且反直觉，相似指令导致不同行为，某些能力无法通过提示激发，人类指令和零样本语言模型难以可靠引导VLA完成任务执行。

Method: 交互式搜索语言序列→蒸馏为测试时语言反馈策略（LFP）→学习改进头预测何时语言引导能提升性能→对改进头进行合规化（conformalize）以防止在分布外场景中LFP降低任务性能。无需微调或访问原始训练分布，适用于任意冻结预训练VLA。

Result: 已见环境：仿真提升24.7%，硬件提升65.0%。在视觉和语义扰动下，合规化LFP具有强无害性保证，产生开环提示无法观察到的恢复行为。

Conclusion: 提出的框架无需微调即可提升冻结VLA模型性能，并有效防止有害语言引导干预，具备分布外场景的安全保障。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide a natural language interface to robot control, but the mapping from language to behavior is often brittle and unintuitive: semantically similar instructions can induce drastically different behaviors, while some capabilities may not be elicitable through prompting alone. As a result, both human instructions and zero-shot language models can fail to reliably steer VLAs toward successful task execution. In this work, we propose a framework that interactively searches for language sequences that improve closed-loop VLA task performance, distills these sequences into a test-time language feedback policy (LFP), and learns an improvement head that predicts when language steering will improve performance. We conformalize this improvement head to prevent harmful steering interventions, where the LFP decreases task performance relative to the original instruction on out-of-distribution scenarios. Crucially, our approach operates on arbitrary frozen pre-trained VLAs, requiring neither access to the original training distribution nor fine-tuning of the underlying model. On seen environments, our conformalized LFP improves base VLA performance by 24.7% in simulation and 65.0% in hardware. On visual and semantic perturbations, our conformalized LFP has strong harmlessness guarantees, and produces recovery behaviors not observed with open-loop prompting.

</details>


### [148] [UGV-Conditioned Multi-UAV Informative Planning on a Shared Exposure Belief](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12306)
*Lars Oerlemans,Moji Shi,Marija Popovic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过多无人机（UAV）协同观测来降低无人地面车辆（UGV）在未知威胁环境中导航风险的方案。核心思路是构建并在线更新共享的暴露置信度图，引导UAV聚焦与UGV路径相关区域进行感知，同时UGV根据新发现的威胁动态重规划。通过空间区域分配避免UAV冗余覆盖，实验显示UGV累积暴露降低38%，冗余覆盖从38.8%降至3.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无人机侦察系统主要关注地图构建或环境覆盖，缺乏对地面车辆安全最相关区域的定向感知。本文旨在解决如何协调多UAV团队主动降低UGV在未知威胁环境中导航风险的问题。

Method: 基于共享暴露置信度更新的在线协同框架：1）UAV根据暴露置信度图将感知聚焦于UGV路径相关区域；2）UGV利用实时观测结果进行路径重规划以规避新发现的威胁；3）通过空间区域分配机制协调多UAV避免冗余感知。

Result: 仿真实验显示：相比不考虑威胁等级的基线系统，UGV累积暴露降低38%；多UAV协调方案将冗余覆盖从38.8%降至3.7%。

Conclusion: 所提出的协同UAV-UGV框架能有效提升未知威胁环境下地面导航的安全性，多UAV协调显著减少了感知冗余。

Abstract: Safe ground navigation in large, threat-augmented environments requires aerial support that actively reduces the risks that a ground vehicle faces along its route. Existing aerial reconnaissance systems focus on mapping or covering the environment, but do not direct sensing toward regions that are most relevant for ground vehicle safety. In this paper, we address the problem of coordinating a team of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to improve the safety of an unmanned ground vehicle (UGV) navigating through unknown threat zones. A key aspect of our approach is a shared exposure belief that is updated online from aerial observations and used jointly by the UAV team and the ground vehicle. This enables us to direct aerial sensing towards route-relevant regions while allowing the UGV to replan around newly revealed threats. We coordinate the UAV team through spatial region assignment to avoid redundant sensing.
